11 INCOME TAX CHARGES (Continued)                                               11 稅項支出（續）
     The Group is subject to the global minimum top-up tax under the             本集團適用經濟合作及發展組織發佈關於支
     Pillar Two model rules published by the Organisation for Economic           柱二全球最低稅率的相關規則。2025年6月，
     Co-operation and Development. In June 2025, the Government                   香港特別行政區政府公告了《2025年稅務（修
     of Hong Kong SAR gazetted the Inland Revenue (Amendment)                     訂）  （跨國企業集團的最低稅）條例》        （「《2025
     (Minimum Tax for Multinational Enterprise Groups) Ordinance 2025             年 修 訂 條 例》」），以 實 施「收 入 包 含 規 則」
     (“the 2025 Amendment Ordinance”) to implement the Income                    （ IIR ）和「香 港 最 低 補 充 稅」（ HKMTT ），這 些
     Inclusion Rule (IIR) and Hong Kong Minimum Top-up Tax (HKMTT),               措 施 對2025年1月1日 或 之 後 開 始 的 財 政 年 度
     which take effect for fiscal years beginning on or after 1 January           生 效。根 據《2025年 修 訂 條 例》，本 集 團 被 視
     2025. According to the 2025 Amendment Ordinance, the Group                   為跨國企業，自2025年1月1日起適用該規則。
     is considered a multinational enterprise to which the rules shall be         根據當前資訊和進行的評估，支柱二法例已生
     applied from 1 January 2025. Based on the information available              效的相關司法管轄區，其對本集團所得稅之當
     and assessment performed, current tax exposure or impact on                  期稅項或影響預期並不重大。
     the Group’s income tax position is not expected to be material for
     the relevant jurisdictions where Pillar Two legislation has become

27 FAIR VALUES OF FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS                                          27 金融工具的公允價值
     (a) Fair value of Group’s financial assets and financial                     (a) 本集團經常性以公允價值計量的金融資
         liabilities that are measured at fair value on a recurring                   產及金融負債的公允價值
         basis

          The fair value of financial assets and liabilities are determined as      金融資產及金融負債的公允價值按以下
          follows:                                                                  方式釐定：

          –    The fair value of financial assets and financial liabilities         –   歸類為第一級為於活躍流動市場交易
               classified as Level 1 with standard terms and conditions                 並受標準條款及條件規管之金融資產
               and traded on active liquid markets are determined with                  及金融負債，其公允價值分別參照近
               reference to recent transaction price or quoted prices                   期交易價格或標價（不做調整）釐定；
               (unadjusted) respectively;

          –    The fair value of derivative instruments are estimated using         –   衍生工具的公允價值乃根據折算現金
               discounted cash flow analysis and the applicable yield                   流分析及對非可選衍生工具的存續期
               curve for the duration of the non-applicable derivative;                 適用收益率曲線釐定；

          –    The fair value of unlisted investment funds and unlisted             –   歸類為第二級為包含在以公允價值計
               debt securities included in financial assets at fair value               量且其變動計入損益及以公允價值計
               through profit or loss and at FVOCI classified as Level                  量且其變動計入其他綜合收益的的金
               2 are established by reference to the prices quoted by                   融資產的非上市投資基金及非上市債
               respective fund administrators or by using valuation                     務投資，其公允價值參照各自的基金
               techniques including discounted cash flow method. The                    管理人報價或採用估值技術包括折
               main parameters used include bond prices, interest rates,                現現金流量法。使用的主要參數包括
               foreign exchange rates, prepayment rates, counter party                  債券價格，利率，外匯匯率，提前償還
               credit spreads and others; and                                           率，對方信用利差及其他；及

          classified as Level 1, fair value is based on quoted prices                       的金融投資，其公允價值乃基於相同資產
          (unadjusted) for identical assets traded in active market.                        於活躍市場中標價（不做調整）釐定。

          interest yield.                                                                   流量分析。

          Level 3, fair value is determined by generally accepted pricing                   務投資，其公允價值按公認定價模式釐定，
          models including discounted cash flow technique by using                          包括基於無法觀察的折現率計算的折算
          unobservable discount rates that reflect the credit risk.                         現金流量分析，以反映相關的信用風險。

          Consequently, the Group concluded that it has a control over            其權力而影響其回報。因此，從2025年1月

          the equity method, and accounted for it as a subsidiary since 1         平金服擁有控制權，不再按權益法的聯營
          January 2025, which is considered as the deemed acquisition             公司核算，並以附屬公司入賬，其資產和
          date. Its assets and liabilities are consolidated into the Group’s      負債以收購法計入本集團合併財務報表。
          consolidated financial statements by using the acquisition
          method.

30 MATERIAL RELATED PARTY TRANSACTIONS                                      30 重大關聯人士交易
     The Group has not entered significant recurring and non-recurring        本集團與關聯人士於本財務期內沒有進行重
     transactions with related parties during the Period.                     大的經常及非經常交易。

     Business transactions between state-owned enterprises controlled         與中國政府控制的國家控股企業（統稱為「國
     by the PRC (collectively “State-Owned Entities”) are within the          有實體」）之間的業務交易屬於關聯交易。本集
     scope of related party transaction. During the Period, the Group         團於本財務期內與國有實體進行包括但不限
     had transactions with State-Owned Entities including but not limited     於保單銷售、租賃服務及銀行相關服務之交易，
     to the sales of insurance policies, leasing services and banking         該等交易所執行的條款跟本集團日常保險業
     related services. These transactions are conducted in the ordinary       務過程中與非國有實體進行交易所執行的條
     course of the Group’s insurance business on terms similar to             款相似。本集團亦已制定就其主要保險產品的
     those that would have been entered into with non-State-Owned             定價策略及審批程序。該等定價策略及審批程
     Entities. The Group has also established its pricing strategy and        序與客戶是否國有實體無關。經考慮其關係的
     approval processes for its major insurance products. Such pricing        性質後，董事相信該等交易並非須獨立披露之
     strategy and approval processes do not depend on whether the             關聯人士交易。
     customers are State-Owned Entities or not. Having due regard to
     the substance of the relationships, the Directors believe that none
     of these transactions are related party transactions that require
     separate disclosure.

          (i)   Market risk                                                         (i)   市場風險

                Market risk can be described as the risk of change in fair                市場風險可以描述為由於利率、股票

                and reinsurance contracts. The nature of the Group’s                      團的業務性質和資產負債匹配流程意

                that it is exposed to market risk on net assets representing              市場風險。本集團保險和投資合同中
                shareholders’ equity. Interest rate risk also arise from                  的擔保也會產生利率風險，只要這些

                (a) Interest rate risk                                                    (a) 利率風險

               Judgement of credit-impaired assets                                 對信用受損資產的判斷

               At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether                  在每個報告日，集團評估以攤餘成本
               financial assets measured at amortised cost, debt                   計量的金融資產、以公允價值計量且
               investments at FVOCI and lease receivables are credit-              其變動計入其他綜合收益的債務投資
               impaired. A financial asset is credit impaired when one             和應收金融租賃款是否信用受損。當
               or more events that have a detrimental impact on the                一個或多個以下事件對金融資產的預
               estimated future cash flows of the financial asset have             期未來現金流造成減少時，該金融資
               occurred.                                                           產的信用受損。

               Evidence that a financial asset is credit-impaired includes         一個金融資產的信用受損的證據包括
               the following observable data:                                      以下可觀測數據：

               –    Internal credit rating is default grade; or                    －   內部信用評級為損失級；或

               –    The lender gives the borrower concessions for                  －   借款人希望債權人因財政困難做
                    economic or contractual reasons due to the debtor                  出經濟上或合約上的讓步，但債
                    financial difficulties, where such concessions are                 權人通常難以接受此讓步；或
                    normally reluctant to be made by the borrower; or

               –    Significant financial difficulty of the issuer or              －   發債人有顯著的財政困難；或
                    counterparty; or

               –    It becoming probable that the borrower will enter              －   借款人有可能破產或重組；或
                    bankruptcy or financial re-organisation; or

               –    Disappearance of an active market for that financial           －   因財政困難，該金融資產不再在
                    asset because of financial difficulties.                           活躍市場上交易。

               A financial asset that has been renegotiated due to                 通常，由於借款人狀況惡化而重新商
               a deterioration in the borrower’s condition is usually              定的金融資產通常被視為信用受損，
               considered to be credit-impaired unless there is evidence           除非有證據表明無法獲得合同現金流
               that the risk of not receiving contractual cash flows has           的風險已大幅降低，並且沒有其他減
               reduced significantly and there are no other indicators of          值跡象。
               impairment.

                    Resort” to that country, as well as the intention,                     以及反映在公開聲明中的政府和

               counterparty fails to make contractual payments within 90               支付合同款項時，該金融資產被視為
               days of when they fall due.                                             信用受損。

               Inputs, assumptions and techniques used for estimating                 用於估計減值的輸入值、假設和技
               impairment                                                             術

               The parameters and assumptions involved in ECL model                   預期信用減值損失模型涉及的參數和
               are described below:                                                   假設描述如下：

               For financial assets with or without significant increase in           對於信用風險有或沒有顯著增加的
               credit risk, lifetime or 12 months expected credit losses              金融資產，分別提供整個期限或12個
               are provided respectively. ECL is the result of discounting            月的預期信用減值損失。預期信用減
               the product of Exposure at Default (“EAD”), Probability at             值損失是對違約風險敞口（「EAD 」）       、
               Default (“PD”) and Loss Given Default (“LGD”).                         違 約 概 率（「 P D 」）和 違 約 損 失 率
                                                                                     （「LGD 」）的乘積進行折現的結果。

               EAD: EAD is based on the amounts of the Group expects                  EAD：EAD是 基 於 在 違 約 時，本 集 團
               to be owed at the time of default, over the next 12 months             在 未 來12個 月 內 或 在 剩 餘 期 限 內 預
               or over the remaining lifetime.                                        期被虧欠的金額。

               PD: PD represents the likelihood of a borrower defaulting              PD：PD代 表 借 款 人 在 未 來12個 月 內
               on its financial obligation, either over the next 12 months or         或在債務的剩餘期限內違約的可能
               over the remaining lifetime of the obligation, depending on            性，這取決於金融資產自初始確認以
               whether the financial asset has significant increase in credit         來信用風險是否大幅增加或被評估為
               risk since initial recognition or is assessed to be credit-            上述的信用受損。每個內部信用評級
               impaired as described above. PD for each internal credit               的PD由集團的信用評級中心確定，並
               rating is determined by the Group’s Credit Rating Center               每年審查。
               and is reviewed annually.

               LGD: LGD represents the Group’s expectation of the                     LGD：LGD代表本集團對違約風險的
               extent of loss on default exposure. LGD varies type of                 損失範圍的預期。LGD因金融資產的
               financial asset, type of counterparty, seniority of claim              類 型、交 易 對 手 的 類 型、賠 款 的 優 先
               and availability of collateral or other credit support. LGD            權和抵押品或其他信貸支持的可用性
               is expressed as a percentage loss per unit of exposure at              而 不 同。LGD以EAD的 每 單 位 風 險 的
               EAD. The Group determines LGD taking into consideration                損失百分比表示。本集團在確定LGD
               publications by Basel Committee on Banking Supervision                 時考慮了巴塞爾銀行監管委員會和穆
               and Moody’s, adjusted based on the financial condition of              迪的出版物，並根據借款人的財務狀
               the borrower and the Group’s experience studies.                       況和本集團的經驗研究進行調整。

               The Group makes adjustment to the probability of default               本集團考慮到歷史違約率，根據宏觀
               taking into consideration historical default rates and                 經濟前瞻性數據，對違約概率進行調
               adjusts for forward-looking macroeconomic data. There                  整。本財務期對於估計調整的技術和
               were no significant changes to estimation techniques or                假設沒有發生重大變化。
               assumptions made during the Period.

          (iii) Liquidity risk                                                                      (iii) 流 動 性 風 險

               The Group has to meet daily calls on its cash resources,                                 本集團須滿足其現金資源的日常調
               notably from claims arising from its life insurance contracts,                           用，特 別 是 來 自 人 壽 保 險 合 同、財 產
               property and casualty insurance contracts and reinsurance                                保險合同以及再保險合同產生的賠款
               contracts. There is, therefore, a risk that cash will not be                             費用。因此存在現金不足以償還到期
               available to settle liabilities when due.                                                負債的風險。

               The Group manages this risk by formulating policies and                                  本集團透過制定流動資金管理的政策
               general strategies of liquidity management to ensure that                                及一般策略來管理該風險，以確保本
               the Group can meet its financial obligations in normal                                   集團在正常情況下能夠履行其財務義
               circumstances and that an adequate stock of high-quality                                 務，並保持足夠的高質量流動性資產
               liquid assets is maintained in order to contain the possibility                          儲備，以應對可能發生的流動資金危
               of a liquidity crisis.                                                                   機。

               A p a r t f ro m l i q u i d i t y m a n a g e m e n t a n d re g u l a t o r y          除了流動資金管理和監管合規外，本
               compliance, the Group always strives to maintain a                                       集團致力於留存適度的流動資金緩衝
               comfortable liquidity cushion as a safety net for coping with                            額，作為應對意料之外的大筆資金需
               unexpected large funding requirements and to maintain                                    求的安全措施，並保持一個應急計劃，
               a contingency plan to be enacted should there be a                                       以便在出現公司特定危機時行動。
               company specific crisis.

Analysis of Investment in Securities                                證券投資的分析

Development on Equity Investments Keep Steady                       權益投資保持穩健

As at the end of June 2025, stock investments held by the           於2025年6月末，本集團持有股票投資約1,399
Group amounted to approximately HK$139.9 billion, representing      億 港 元，佔 總 資 產 比 例 約7.5%。本 集 團 堅 持
approximately 7.5% of the total assets. Adhering to the concept     價值投資理念，發揮保險資金長期優勢，主要
of value investment, our Group has leveraged on the long-term       投向估值合理、分紅穩定，具有較好安全邊際
advantages of insurance funds and mainly invested in equities       的品種，投資風格總體保持穩健。
with reasonable valuation, stable dividends and good margin of
safety, and the overall investment style remained prudent.

High Credit Ratings for Debt Securities                             債務證券信用評級較高

CAUTIONARY STATEMENT                                                   提示聲明

The calculations of Embedded Value and the New Business Value          計算太平人壽的內含價值及新業務價值乃基
of TPL are based on certain assumptions with respect to future         於有關未來經驗之若干假設。故此實際結果
experience. Thus, the actual results could differ significantly from   可能與作出該等計算時之預測有重大差異。
what were envisioned when these calculations were made. In             此外，總內含價值亦基於若干假設，因此不應
addition, the Group Embedded Value is also based on certain            視之為評價及評估本集團業務營運的唯一基
assumptions, and should not be viewed as the only benchmark            準。從投資者角度看，中國太平控股之估值乃
for evaluating and valuing the businesses and operations of the        按照本公司股份於某個別日子之股市價格計
Group. From an investor’s perspective, the valuation of CTIH is        量。於評估中國太平控股股份時，投資者不僅
measured by the stock market price of the Company’s shares             要慮及太平人壽的內含價值及新業務價值和
on any particular day. In valuing CTIH’s shares, investors should      總內含價值，而且亦應考慮到其他多項因素。
take into account not only the Embedded Value and the New              此外，本公司擁有太平人壽之75.1%股權。因
Business Value of TPL and the Group Embedded Value, but also           此，不應把下列所披露之於2025年6月30日之
various other considerations. In addition, TPL is 75.1%-owned          太平人壽內含價值及新業務價值全數作為中
by the Company. The Embedded Value and the New Business                國太平控股的估值。倘若彼等認為該等因素
Value of TPL as at 30 June 2025 as disclosed below should              重要，及對本公司之估值關係重大，投資者務
therefore not be applied 100% in valuing CTIH. Investors are           須特別留意該等因素，及其他支持計算太平
advised to pay particular attention to this factor, as well as the     人壽內含價值及新業務價值計算及總內含價

INTRODUCTION                                                         引言

We have reviewed the interim financial report set out on             我 們 審 閱 了 列 載 於 第3至75頁 的 中 國 太 平 保
pages 3 to 75, which comprises the condensed consolidated 險 控 股 有 限 公 司（「貴 公 司」）及 其 附 屬 公 司
statement of financial position of China Taiping Insurance （統稱「貴集團」）的中期財務報告，包括2025
Holdings Company Limited (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries 年6月30日的簡明綜合財務狀況表、截至2025
(collectively referred to as the “Group”) as of 30 June 2025 and 年6月30日止六個月的簡明綜合損益表、簡明
the related condensed consolidated statement of profit or loss, 綜合損益及其他綜合收益表、簡明綜合權益
condensed consolidated statement of profit or loss and other 變動表和簡明綜合現金流量表，以及財務報
comprehensive income, condensed consolidated statement of 告附註。香港聯合交易所有限公司證券上市
changes in equity and condensed consolidated statement of            規則要求按照其相關規定和香港會計師公會
cash flows for the six-month period then ended and explanatory 頒布的香港會計準則第34號「中期財務報告」
notes. The Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on The 的要求編製中期財務報告。按照香港會計準
Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited require the preparation of 則第34號「中期財務報告」的要求編製和列報
an interim financial report to be in compliance with the relevant 上述中期財務報告是貴公司董事的責任。
provisions thereof and Hong Kong Accounting Standard 34
Interim financial reporting as issued by the Hong Kong Institute
of Certified Public Accountants. The directors are responsible for
the preparation and presentation of this interim financial report in
accordance with Hong Kong Accounting Standard 34.

Our responsibility is to express a conclusion, based on our          我們的責任是在實施審閱工作的基礎上對上
review, on this interim financial report and to report our           述中期財務報告發表審閱意見。根據雙方已

Report on Review of Interim Financial Information
中期財務資料的審閱報告

SCOPE OF REVIEW                                                       審閱工作範圍

We conducted our review in accordance with Hong Kong                  我們的審閱工作是按照香港會計師公會頒布
Standard on Review Engagements 2410, Review of interim                的 香 港 審 閱 準 則 第2410號「獨 立 核 數 師 對 企
financial information performed by the independent auditor of the     業中期財務信息執行審閱」的要求進行的。中
entity as issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public       期財務報告的審閱工作主要包括向財務會計
Accountants. A review of interim financial report consists of         負責人進行查詢，執行分析性覆核及其他審
making inquiries, primarily of persons responsible for financial      閱程序。由於審閱的範圍遠小於按照香港審
and accounting matters, and applying analytical and other review      計準則進行審計的範圍，所以不能保證我們
procedures. A review is substantially less in scope than an audit     會注意到在審計中可能會被發現的所有重大
conducted in accordance with Hong Kong Standards on Auditing          事項。因此，我們不發表審計意見。
and consequently does not enable us to obtain assurance that
we would become aware of all significant matters that might be
identified in an audit. Accordingly we do not express an audit
opinion.


CONCLUSION                                                            結論

Based on our review, nothing has come to our attention that           根據我們的審閱，我們沒有注意到任何事項
causes us to believe that the interim financial report of the Group   使 我 們 相 信 於2025年6月30日 的 中 期 財 務 報
as at 30 June 2025 is not prepared, in all material respects, in      告在所有重大方面沒有按照香港會計準則第
accordance with Hong Kong Accounting Standard 34, Interim             34號「中期財務報告」編製。
financial reporting.
